# 24.11.13 에너지프라자 신규 거래처 코드도움 생성

DROP procedure SLAHelpCodeQuery

GO

/\*

-- 코드헬프용 sp

SLAHelpCodeQuery_TTEST 'S24'

 

Select \* from TDAItemUnit

 

수정내용 : 사이트용으로 같은 constant를 사용하여 필요한 것이 있어서 수정작업을 함

예를들어 'CST'를 사이트용으로 만들경우 'CST'를 사용하는 메인 화면을 모두 수정할 수 없으므로....

수정자 : 홍기화

수정일 : 2003.12.16

수정일 : 2008.03.31 (정동혁) 코드헬프창에서 @iBizUnit를 받아오지 않으므로 품목코드헬프에서 @iBizUnit를 제외시킴.

수정일 : 2010.05.27 (김은정) @iTMajorCode = 'COT' 다국어처리.

\*/

 

create procedure SLAHelpCodeQuery

@iTMajorCode char(3) = null, /\* Major Code \*/

@iSortOrder tinyint = null, /\* Sort Order 0:명칭순 1:코드순 \*/

@iOperator tinyint = null, /\* Operator 0:Equal 1:Equal Or Greater \*/

@iStartCode NVARCHAR(100) = null, /\* StartCode \*/

@iQryCond1 NVARCHAR(30) = null, /\* 조회 조건1 \*/

@iQryCond2 NVARCHAR(30) = null, /\* 조회 조건2 \*/

@iQryCond3 NVARCHAR(30) = null, /\* 조회 조건3 \*/

@iQryCond4 NVARCHAR(30) = null, /\* 조회 조건4 \*/

@iQryCond5 NVARCHAR(30) = null, /\* 조회 조건5 \*/

@iSubMajorCode char(3) = null, /\* Sub Major Code \*/

@iPrev tinyint = null, /\* 0: asc 1: desc \*/

@i1 varchar(30) = null,

@i2 varchar(30) = null,

@i3 varchar(30) = null,

@i4 varchar(30) = null,

@i5 varchar(30) = null,

@i6 varchar(30) = null,

@i7 varchar(30) = null,

@i8 varchar(30) = null,

@i9 varchar(30) = null,

@i10 varchar(30) = null,

@iRowCount int = null,

@iBizUnit char(2) = '01',

@iIsReCall varchar(1) = 'S' -- VB에서 call시에는 값이 없음(S), 사이트용 sp에서 call시는 'R'

/\*

with encryption

\*/

AS

declare @iMajorCode char(3), @wSubSql varchar(100), @wSql NVARCHAR(4000),@nCnt int, @vcQryCond5Flag varchar(1),

@iMajorCnst char(2),

@wDeptCd char(5), @wZoneNo varchar(5), @wTitleNm varchar(20), @wItemKind varchar(1),@vcSiteInit varchar(40), @cIsBiz char(1), @vcCostType char(4), @vcCostType1 char(4),

@cUseTruncNmYn char(1),

@cQryCond1 char(30), /\* 조회 조건1 \*/

@cQryCond2 char(30), /\* 조회 조건2 \*/

@cQryCond3 char(30), /\* 조회 조건3 \*/

@cQryCond4 char(30), /\* 조회 조건4 \*/

@cQryCond5 char(30) /\* 조회 조건5 \*/

select @iMajorCode = @iTMajorCode,

-- @iStartCode = isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@iStartCode)), ''),

@iStartCode = isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@iStartCode)), ''),

@iQryCond1 = isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@iQryCond1)), ''),

@iQryCond2 = isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@iQryCond2)), ''),

@iQryCond3 = isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@iQryCond3)), ''),

@iQryCond4 = isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@iQryCond4)), ''),

@iQryCond5 = isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@iQryCond5)), ''),

@iSubMajorCode = isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@iSubMajorCode)), ''),

@i1=isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@i1)), ''),

@i2=isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@i2)), ''),

@i3=isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@i3)), ''),

@i4=isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@i4)), ''),

@i5=isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@i5)), ''),

@i6=isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@i6)), ''),

@i7=isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@i7)), ''),

@i8=isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@i8)), ''),

@i9=isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@i9)), ''),

@i10=isnull(ltrim(rtrim(@i10)), '')

SELECT @cQryCond1 = @iQryCond1,

@cQryCond2 = @iQryCond2,

@cQryCond3 = @iQryCond3,

@cQryCond4 = @iQryCond4,

@cQryCond5 = @iQryCond5

>  

-- 고객 코드도움시 삭제된 고객은 조회가 안되도록 체크하기 위한 소분류값(상태:삭제) 2009.01.02 jhkong

DECLARE @vcCustStatusCheck CHAR(8)

> SET @vcCustStatusCheck = ''

SELECT @vcCustStatusCheck = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(MinorCd, ''))) FROM TDAMinor WHERE MinorCd Like '183%' And Item4 = '1'

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- 제품 CodeHelp인 경우는 재고자산 분류별 코드도움유형을 선택한다.

-- 앞의 두자리로 구분한다 2001.01.10. 김진환

Select @iMajorCnst = Left(@iMajorCode, 2)

-- TruncNm 사용여부를 체크한다. 2007.01.26

SELECT @cUseTruncNmYn = ISNULL(UseTruncNm, '0')

FROM TCTCodeHelpMajor

WHERE MajorCd = @iMajorCode

SELECT @cUseTruncNmYn = ISNULL(@cUseTruncNmYn, '0')

IF @iMajorCnst = 'AT' Or @iMajorCnst = 'BT' Or @iMajorCnst = 'PT' Or @iMajorCnst = 'XJ' Or @iMajorCnst = 'XP' Or

@iMajorCnst = 'JT' Or @iMajorCnst = 'GT' Or @iMajorCnst = 'OT' Or @iMajorCnst = 'OI' Or @iMajorCnst = 'PI' Or @iMajorCnst = 'EX'

BEGIN

 

>  
>
> IF @iQryCond4 = 'frmSASalesReturn'
>
> BEGIN
>
> SELECT @i10 = @iQryCond5
>
> SELECT @iQryCond4 = '', @iQryCond5 = ''
>
>  
>
> END

CREATE Table \#TempAssetType

(

AssetType char(1)

)

Insert Into \#TempAssetType

Select Item1

From TDAMinor with (nolock)

Where MinorCd like '099%'

And ((Case @iMajorCnst WHEN 'BT' THEN Item8

WHEN 'AT' THEN Item2 --Item9 (판매여부 체크에서 제품/상품 체크여부로변경 20090922)

WHEN 'PT' THEN Item10

WHEN 'JT' THEN Item11

WHEN 'GT' THEN Item9

WHEN 'OT' THEN Item12

WHEN 'PI' THEN Item10

WHEN 'OI' THEN Item12

WHEN 'XP' THEN Item10 -- 제품/반제품(옵션처리)

WHEN 'XJ' THEN Item11 -- 자재 Assey제외

END) = '1'

OR

(@iMajorCnst = 'EX' AND Item2 = '0')) -- 제품/상품이 아닌 것(기타입출고용)

END

-- 'XJ'인것은 자재중에서 Assey인것을 빼기 위한 옵션등록의 코드도움상수이다. (2006/01/10 jhkong)

If @iMajorCnst = 'XJ'

Delete \#TempAssetType Where AssetType = '5'

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

if @iRowCount is null select @iRowCount = 0

set rowcount @iRowCount

-- sub조건이 없어도 Default값을 넣어주기 위해...

select @wSubSql = '(1=1)'

-- sub조건이 있으면 Sub조건을 넣어줌

if @iSubMajorCode != ''

select @wSubSql = rtrim(ltrim(SubMajorSql)) from TLACodeHelpSubMajor with (nolock) where MajorCd = @iMajorCode and SubMajorCd = @iSubMajorCode

--

select @wSql = ''

/\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*/

if @iTMajorCode = 'CST' or @iTMajorCode = 'CSS' or @iTMajorCode = 'CNO' --거래처

begin

select @cIsBiz = Value from TDAControlSub where SubSeq = '002' and Seq = '004' -- 거래처를 사업장별로 관리

if @@rowcount = 0 or @cIsBiz = '' select @cIsBiz = '0'

if @iTMajorCode = 'CST' -- 20080905 by Him 엠텍요청으로 주민번호는 안보여주게 수정 --20110911 김준식 사업자번호 연우 요청으로 '-'표시

select @wSql = "select cust.CustNm,SUBString(cust.Bizno_Nation,1,3)+'-'+SUBString(cust.Bizno_Nation,4,2)+'-'+SUBString(cust.Bizno_Nation,6,5) AS Bizno_Nation,cust.CustCd,cust.CustCd,cust.TrunNm, '' AS PersonId_TelexNo ,cust.CustomerNo,cust.Owner,cust.BizAddr,'','','',''

from TDACustMaster cust With (NoLock) left outer join TDAMinor mnr With (NoLock) on ( cust.CustStatus = mnr.MinorCd)

where ( '" + @cIsBiz + "' = '0' or cust.AccUnit = '"+ @iBizUnit + "' )" + "

and ( (Isnull(mnr.Item3,'') \<\> '1' and Isnull(mnr.Item4,'') \<\> '1') or cust.CustStatus = '') " -- 신규거래처 제외

else

select @wSql = "select cust.CustomerNo,cust.CustNm,cust.CustCd,cust.CustCd,cust.CustomerNo,SUBString(cust.Bizno_Nation,1,3)+'-'+SUBString(cust.Bizno_Nation,4,2)+'-'+SUBString(cust.Bizno_Nation,6,5) AS Bizno_Nation, '' AS PersonId_TelexNo ,cust.Owner,'','','','',''

from TDACustMaster cust with (nolock) left outer join TDAMinor mnr with (nolock) on ( cust.CustStatus = mnr.MinorCd)

where ( '" + @cIsBiz + "' = '0' or cust.AccUnit = '"+ @iBizUnit + "' )" + "

and ( (Isnull(mnr.Item3,'') \<\> '1' and Isnull(mnr.Item4,'') \<\> '1') or cust.CustStatus = '') " -- 신규거래처 제외

/\*

if @iTMajorCode = 'CST'

select @wSql = "select cust.CustNm,cust.Bizno_Nation,cust.CustCd,cust.CustCd,cust.TrunNm,cust.PersonId_TelexNo,cust.CustomerNo,cust.Owner,'','','','',''

from TDACustMaster cust With (NoLock) left outer join TDAMinor mnr With (NoLock) on ( cust.CustStatus = mnr.MinorCd)

where ( (Isnull(mnr.Item3,'') \<\> '1' and Isnull(mnr.Item4,'') \<\> '1') or cust.CustStatus = '') " -- 신규거래처 제외

else

select @wSql = "select cust.CustomerNo,cust.CustNm,cust.CustCd,cust.CustCd,cust.CustomerNo,cust.Bizno_Nation,cust.PersonId_TelexNo,cust.Owner,'','','','',''

from TDACustMaster cust with (nolock) left outer join TDAMinor mnr with (nolock) on ( cust.CustStatus = mnr.MinorCd)

where ( (Isnull(mnr.Item3,'') \<\> '1' and Isnull(mnr.Item4,'') \<\> '1') or cust.CustStatus = '') " -- 신규거래처 제외

\*/

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

begin

if @iTMajorCode = 'CNO'

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustomerNo \<= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " \<= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

end

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

begin

if @iTMajorCode = 'CNO'

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustomerNo like N'"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " like N'"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

end

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

begin

if @iTMajorCode = 'CNO'

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustomerNo \>= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " \>= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

end

END

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + @wSubSql

if @i1 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustNm like N'" + @i1 +"%' "

if @i2 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.Bizno_Nation like N'" + @i2 +"%' "

if @i3 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd like '" + @i3 +"%' "

if @i4 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd like '" + @i4 +"%' "

if @i5 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.TrunNm like N'" + @i5 +"%' "

if @i6 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and dbo.\_FCOMDecrypt(cust.PersonId_TelexNo, 'TDACustMaster' , 'PersonId_TelexNo', 1) like '" + @i6 +"%' "

if @i7 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustomerNo like '" + @i7 +"%' "

if @iPrev = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by cust.CustCd "

else

begin

if @iTMajorCode = 'CNO'

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by cust.CustomerNo"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END

end

end

else

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by cust.CustCd desc "

else

begin

if @iTMajorCode = 'CNO'

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by cust.CustomerNo desc"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " desc"

end

end

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

end

-- 신규거래처 포함 (경영계획) 추가 by 홍기화

if @iTMajorCode = 'CNW' --신규거래처

begin

select @wSql = "select cust.CustNm,cust.Bizno_Nation,cust.CustCd,cust.CustCd,cust.TrunNm,dbo.\_FCOMDecrypt(cust.PersonId_TelexNo, 'TDACustMaster' , 'PersonId_TelexNo', 1),cust.CustomerNo,cust.Owner,'','','','',''

from TDACustMaster cust With (NoLock) left outer join TDAMinor mnr With (NoLock) on ( cust.CustStatus = mnr.MinorCd)

where ( Isnull(mnr.Item4,'') \<\> '1' or cust.CustStatus = '') " -- 신규거래처 제외

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " \<= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " like N'"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " \>= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

END

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + @wSubSql

if @i1 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustNm like '" + @i1 +"%' "

if @i2 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.Bizno_Nation like '" + @i2 +"%' "

if @i3 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd like '" + @i3 +"%' "

if @i4 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd like '" + @i4 +"%' "

if @i5 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.TrunNm like '" + @i5 +"%' "

if @i6 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and dbo.\_FCOMDecrypt(cust.PersonId_TelexNo, 'TDACustMaster' , 'PersonId_TelexNo', 1) like '" + @i6 +"%' "

if @i7 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustomerNo like '" + @i7 +"%' "

if @iPrev = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by cust.CustCd "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " "

end

else

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by cust.CustCd desc "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + ""

end

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

end

 

-- 삭제거래처 포함 24.11.13 SJPARK

if @iTMajorCode = 'CSD' -- 삭제거래처

begin

select @wSql = "select cust.CustNm,cust.Bizno_Nation,cust.CustCd,cust.CustCd,cust.TrunNm,dbo.\_FCOMDecrypt(cust.PersonId_TelexNo, 'TDACustMaster' , 'PersonId_TelexNo', 1),cust.CustomerNo,cust.Owner,'','','','',''

from TDACustMaster cust With (NoLock) left outer join TDAMinor mnr With (NoLock) on ( cust.CustStatus = mnr.MinorCd)

where ( 1=1 ) " -- 삭제거래처 조회되도록 거래처상태값 조건 제거

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " \<= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " like N'"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " \>= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

END

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + @wSubSql

if @i1 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustNm like '" + @i1 +"%' "

if @i2 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.Bizno_Nation like '" + @i2 +"%' "

if @i3 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd like '" + @i3 +"%' "

if @i4 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustCd like '" + @i4 +"%' "

if @i5 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.TrunNm like '" + @i5 +"%' "

if @i6 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and dbo.\_FCOMDecrypt(cust.PersonId_TelexNo, 'TDACustMaster' , 'PersonId_TelexNo', 1) like '" + @i6 +"%' "

if @i7 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and cust.CustomerNo like '" + @i7 +"%' "

if @iPrev = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by cust.CustCd "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + " "

end

else

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by cust.CustCd desc "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " cust.TrunNm " ELSE " cust.CustNm " END + ""

end

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

end

 

 

/\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*/

-- CRM Module의 고객

if @iTMajorCode = 'CMR'

begin

select @wSql= "select CustNm,BizOrPersonNo,CustID,CustID,CustSNm,'','',CustType,'','','','',''

from TRACust with (nolock) "

if @iPrev = 1

BEGIN

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " where CustID \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " where CustSNm \<= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

END

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " where CustID like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " where CustSNm like N'"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " where CustID \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " where CustSNm \>= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

END

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + @wSubSql

/\*

if @i1 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and CustID like '" + @i1 +"%' "

if @i2 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and BizOrPersonNo like '" + @i2 +"%' "

if @i3 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and CustID like '" + @i3 +"%' "

begin

if ltrim(rtrim(@iQryCond3)) = '1' -- 구매단가기준단위 적용여부

Select @wSql = "Select Case When B.PurPriceUnitYN = '1' Then M2.UnitNm Else M.UnitNm End, M.Remark ,

Case When B.PurPriceUnitYN = '1' Then B.PurPriceUnitCd Else I.UnitCd End, Case When B.PurPriceUnitYN = '1' Then B.PurPriceUnitCd Else I.UnitCd End,

Case When B.PurPriceUnitYN = '1' Then M2.UnitNm Else M.UnitNm End, I.ConvQty, '' , I.ItemCd, '" + ltrim(rtrim(@iQryCond2)) + "', '' ,'','','' "

\+ "From TDAItemUnit I With (NoLock) "

\+ " Join TDAItemMaster as B On I.ItemCd = B.ItemCd And I.UnitCd = Case When B.PurPriceUnitYN = '1' Then B.PurPriceUnitCd Else I.UnitCd End "

\+ " Left Outer Join TDAUnit M With (NoLock) On I.UnitCd = M.UnitCd"

\+ " Left Outer Join TDAUnit M2 With (NoLock) On B.PurPriceUnitCd = M2.UnitCd"

Else

Select @wSql = "select M.UnitNm ,M.Remark ,I.UnitCd, I.UnitCd, M.UnitNm, I.ConvQty, '' , I.ItemCd, '" + ltrim(rtrim(@iQryCond2)) + "', '' ,'','','' "

\+ "From TDAItemUnit I With (NoLock) "

\+ " Left Outer Join TDAUnit M With (NoLock) On I.UnitCd = M.UnitCd"

Select @wSql = @wSql + " Where I.ItemCd = '" + ltrim(rtrim(@iQryCond1)) + "'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and U.UnitNm \<= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and I.UnitCd like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and M.UnitNm like N'"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and I.ItemCd \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and M.UnitNm \>= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

if @iPrev = 0

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by I.UnitCd "

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by M.UnitNm "

end

else

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by I.UnitCd desc "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by M.UnitNm desc"

end

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

---- 폼

begin

Select @i1 = '0'

select @wSql = "select '', IsNull(b.DicName, a.Caption), a.PgmID, a.PgmID, c.AffairName, '', '', '' "

\+ " , '', '' ,'','','' "

\+ " from TCTPgmCntlM a with (nolock) left outer join TCTDictionaryCommon b with (nolock) "

\+ " on ( a.PgmID = b.DicCode And b.LanguageID = " + @i1 + " ) "

\+ " left outer join TCTAffair c with (nolock) "

\+ " on ( a.Affair = c.Affair ) "

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " where a.PgmID \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " where IsNull(b.DicName, a.Caption) \<= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " where a.PgmID like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " where IsNull(b.DicName, a.Caption) like N'"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " where a.PgmID \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " where IsNull(b.DicName, a.Caption) \>= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

END

if @iPrev = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by a.PgmID "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by IsNull(b.DicName, a.Caption) "

end

else

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

else if @iTMajorCode = 'COT' --원가항목(추가 by 홍기화:2000.03.16)

begin

select @cIsBiz = Value from TDAControlSub where SubSeq = '003' and Seq = '004' -- 품목을 사업장별로 관리

if @@rowcount = 0 or @cIsBiz = '' select @cIsBiz = '0'

--운영환경관리(생산원가)-초기 배부기준 품목분류 선택(원가)에 따라 보여지도록 by 김영우(2006.03.21)

select @vcCostType = case Value

when '0' then '040%' --품목분류1

when '1' then '041%' --품목분류2

when '2' then '042%' --품목분류3

when '3' then '043%' --품목분류4

else '040%' end

from TDAControlSub with (nolock) where SubSeq = '001' and Seq = '451'

if @@rowcount = 0 or @vcCostType = '' select @vcCostType = '040%'

--운영환경관리(생산원가)-초기 배부기준 품목분류 선택(판관비)에 따라 보여지도록 by 김영우(2006.03.21)

select @vcCostType1 = case Value

when '0' then '040%' --품목분류1

when '1' then '041%' --품목분류2

when '2' then '042%' --품목분류3

when '3' then '043%' --품목분류4

else '040%' end

from TDAControlSub with (nolock) where SubSeq = '001' and Seq = '452'

if @@rowcount = 0 or @vcCostType1 = '' select @vcCostType1 = '040%'

select @wSql = "select sub.nm, sub.rmk, sub.cd, sub.als, sub.tnm, sub.Info1,'','','','','','',''

from (

(select N" + N'''공통''' +" as nm, N" + N'''공통''' + " as rmk, 'D' as cd, 'D' as als, N" + N'''공통''' + " as tnm, '' AS Info1) --공통

union

(select MinorNm as nm,Remark as rmk,'P'+MinorCd as cd,'P'+MinorCd as als,TrunNm as tnm, '' AS Info1

from TDAMinor with (nolock) where MinorCd like 'A42%') -- 프로젝트

union

(select MinorNm as nm,Remark as rmk,'C'+MinorCd as cd,'C'+MinorCd as als,TrunNm as tnm, '' AS Info1

--from TDAMinor with (nolock) where MinorCd like '040%') --품목그룹(품목분류1)

from TDAMinor with (nolock) where MinorCd like '"+@vcCostType+ "' or MinorCd like '"+@vcCostType1+ "')

union

(select ItemNm as nm,ItemNo as rmk,'I'+ItemCd as cd,'I'+ItemCd as als,TrunNm as tnm, Spec AS Info1

from TDAItemMaster with (nolock)

where (AssetType = '1' OR AssetType = '2' or AssetType = '4') -- 제품, 반제품에서 상품도 포함 2008.10.28 김동현

and ('" + @cIsBiz + "' = '0' or AccUnit = '"+ @iBizUnit + "' ) ) " + " --품목

union

(select N" + N'''부서''' + " as nm, N" + N'''부서''' + " as rmk, 'E' as cd, 'E' as als, N" + N'''부서''' + " as tnm, '' AS Info1) -- 부서

union

(select CustNm as nm,Remark as rmk,'T'+CustCd as cd,'T'+CustCd as als,TrunNm as tnm, '' AS Info1

from TDACustMaster with (nolock) ) -- 거래처

union

(select MinorNm as nm,Remark as rmk,'S'+MinorCd as cd,'S'+MinorCd as als,TrunNm as tnm, '' AS Info1

from TDAMinor with (nolock) where MinorCd like '002%') -- 판매유형

) sub "

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " where sub.cd \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " where sub.nm \<= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " where sub.cd like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " where sub.nm like N'"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " where sub.cd \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " where sub.nm \>= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

END

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + @wSubSql

if @i1 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and sub.nm like '" + @i1 +"%' "

if @i2 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and sub.rmk like '" + @i2 +"%' "

if @i3 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and sub.cd like '" + @i3 +"%' "

if @i4 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and sub.als like '" + @i4 +"%' "

if @i5 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and sub.tnm like '" + @i5 +"%' "

if @iPrev = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by sub.cd "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by sub.nm "

end

else

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by sub.cd desc "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by sub.nm desc"

end

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

end

/\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*/

else if @iTMajorCode = 'CTM' -- 고객전부

begin

-- select @wSql = "select A.CustNm, A.CustNo, isnull(P.CompanyNm, ''), A.CustSeq, A.TrunNm,

select @wSql = "select A.CustNm, A.CustNo, A.CustSeq, A.CustSeq, A.TrunNm,

A.CustType as CustType2, A.KoOrFo as KoOrFo, A.CustKind as CustKind, A.StatusType as StatusType, '1',

Case A.CustType when 'Z0100003' then '1' else '0' End as CustType,

'',''

From TECCustM A with (nolock)

left outer join TECCustCompany P with (nolock)

on A.CustSeq = P.CustSeq and P.ToDate = '99991231'

left outer join TECCustTel T with (nolock) On A.CustSeq = T.CustSeq And T.MajorYN = '1'

Where 1 = 1 And (IsNull(A.Status, '') = '' OR A.Status \<\> '" + @vcCustStatusCheck + "')"

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " And A.CustSeq \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " And " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " A.TrunNm " ELSE " A.CustNm " END + " \<= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " And A.CustSeq like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " And " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " A.TrunNm " ELSE " A.CustNm " END + " like N'"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " And A.CustSeq \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " And " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " A.TrunNm " ELSE " A.CustNm " END + " \>= N'"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

END

-- 이하 정보권한 Check

if ltrim(rtrim(@iQryCond3)) != ''

begin

select @wDeptCd = isnull(DeptCd, '') from TECCustCompany with (nolock) where CustSeq = @iQryCond3 and ToDate = '99991231'

select @wDeptCd = Case isnull(@wDeptCd, '') when '' then '99999' else @wDeptCd end

--or (A.SecuType = 'Z0400002' and (A.MngDeptCd = " + @wDeptCd + " or A.RegCustSeq = " + @iQryCond3 + "))

or (A.SecuType = 'Z0400003') or (A.SecuType = '')) "

end

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + @wSubSql

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by A.CustSeq "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " A.TrunNm " ELSE " A.CustNm " END + " "

end

else

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by A.CustSeq desc "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " A.TrunNm " ELSE " A.CustNm " END + " desc"

end

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

A.ActorId, A.ActorId, IsNull(Rtrim(Ltrim(C.MinorNm)), '') as Grade, A.GradeCd,

else

else if @iTMajorCode = 'CDN' -- 고객,기업,직원

begin

select @wSql = "select A.CustNo, A.CustNm, A.CustSeq, A.CustSeq, A.CustNo,

Case B.CompanyCd when '' then B.CompanyNm Else IsNull(C.CustNm, '') End as CompanyNm,

IsNull(B.CompanyCd, '') as CompanyCd ,

'"+@iQryCond1+"' as QueryCond ,

Case B.DeptCd when '' then B.DeptNm Else IsNull(D.DeptNm, '') End as DeptNm,

IsNull(B.DeptCd, '') as DeptCd ,

Case A.CustType when 'Z0100003' then '1' else '0' End as CustType,

'',''

From TECCustM A with (nolock) left outer join TECCustCompany B with (nolock) on A.CustSeq = B.CustSeq And B.ToDate like '9999%'

left outer join TECCustM C with (nolock) on B.CompanyCd = C.CustSeq

left outer join TDADept D with (nolock) on B.DeptCd = D.DeptCd --And D.EndDate = ''

if @iSortOrder = 1

else if @iQryCond1 = '3'

if ltrim(rtrim(@iQryCond3)) != ''

begin

select @wDeptCd = isnull(DeptCd, '') from TECCustCompany with (nolock) where CustSeq = @iQryCond3 and ToDate = '99991231'

select @wDeptCd = Case isnull(@wDeptCd, '') when '' then '99999' else @wDeptCd end

--or (A.SecuType = 'Z0400002' and (A.MngDeptCd = " + @wDeptCd + " or A.RegCustSeq = " + @iQryCond3 + "))

or (A.SecuType = 'Z0400003') or (A.SecuType = '')) "

end

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by A.CustSeq "

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by A.CustNo "

end

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by A.CustSeq desc "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by A.CustNo desc"

end

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

/\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*/

-- 계획생산에서 사용 - 년월주차 시작 2007.02.16 김동현

/\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*/

IF @iMajorCode = 'XWK'

BEGIN

select @wSql = "select c.RptCycleSerl, c.RptCycleSerl, c.RptCycleSerl

, convert(char(10), convert(datetime,c.RptCycleStartDate), 120) as RptCycleStartDate

, convert(char(10), convert(datetime,c.RptCycleEndDate), 120) as RptCycleEndDate, '', '', '', '', '', '', ''

from TEAReportCycle c

where c.RptCycleKind = '2' "

-- sub조건이 있으면 Sub조건을 넣어줌

select @wSubSql = '(1=1)'

if @iSubMajorCode != ''

select @wSubSql = rtrim(ltrim(SubMajorSql)) from TLACodeHelpSubMajor where MajorCd = @iMajorCode and SubMajorCd = @iSubMajorCode

if @iRowCount is null select @iRowCount = 0

set rowcount @iRowCount

--

/\* 0: asc 1: desc \*/

if @iPrev = 1

begin

/\* Sort Order 0:명칭순 1:코드순 \*/

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and replace(c.RptCycleSerl,'-','') \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and replace(c.RptCycleSerl,'-','') \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

else

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and replace(c.RptCycleSerl,'-','') like '"+ replace(@iStartCode,'-','') +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and replace(c.RptCycleSerl,'-','') like '"+ replace(@iStartCode,'-','') +"%'"

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and replace(c.RptCycleSerl,'-','') \>= '"+ replace(@iStartCode,'-','') +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and replace(c.RptCycleSerl,'-','') \>= '"+ replace(@iStartCode,'-','') +"'"

end

END

select @wSql = @wSql + " and " + @wSubSql

if @i1 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and c.RptCycleSerl like '" + @i1 +"%' "

if @i2 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and c.RptCycleStartDate like '" + @i2 +"%' "

if @i3 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and c.RptCycleEndDate like '" + @i3 +"%' "

if @iPrev = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by c.RptCycleSerl "

else

begin

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by c.RptCycleSerl "

end

end

else

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by c.RptCycleSerl desc "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by c.RptCycleSerl desc"

end

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

END

if @iTMajorCode = '304' -- 부서를 사용하지 않는 부서는 안보이는 것이 기본으로 하기 위해

begin

select @wSql = "select MinorNm,Remark,JoinTable,JoinTable,TrunNm,

Item1,Item2,Item3,Item4,Item5,Item6,Item7,''

from TDAMinor with (nolock)

where "

if @iSubMajorCode != ''

select @wSubSql = rtrim(ltrim(isnull(SubMajorSql,"1=1"))) from TLACodeHelpSubMajor where MajorCd = @iMajorCode and SubMajorCd = @iSubMajorCode

else

select @wSubSql = " rtrim(ltrim(Item1)) ='1' "

select @wSql = @wSql + @wSubSql

if @iPrev = 1

begin

select @wSql = @wSql + " and MinorCd like '"+ @iTMajorCode +"%' and JoinTable \<= '" + @iStartCode +"'

and ('" + @iQryCond1+ "'='' or Item3 = '" + @iQryCond1 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond2+ "'='' or Item4 = '" + @iQryCond2 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond3+ "'='' or Item5 = '" + @iQryCond3 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond1+ "'='' or Item3 = '" + @iQryCond1 + "')

BEGIN

and ('" + @iQryCond2+ "'='' or Item4 = '" + @iQryCond2 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond5+ "'='' or a.Item7 = '" + @iQryCond5 + "')

and " + @wSubSql

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.MinorCd like '"+ @iTMajorCode +"%' and a.JoinTable \>= '" + @iStartCode +"'

and ('" + @iQryCond1+ "'='' or a.Item3 = '" + @iQryCond1 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond2+ "'='' or a.Item4 = '" + @iQryCond2 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond3+ "'='' or a.Item5 = '" + @iQryCond3 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond4+ "'='' or a.Item6 = '" + @iQryCond4 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond5+ "'='' or a.Item7 = '" + @iQryCond5 + "')

and " + @wSubSql

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.MinorCd like '"+ @iTMajorCode +"%' and " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " a.TrunNm " ELSE " a.MinorNm " END + " \>= N'" + @iStartCode +"'

and ('" + @iQryCond1+ "'='' or a.Item3 = '" + @iQryCond1 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond2+ "'='' or a.Item4 = '" + @iQryCond2 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond3+ "'='' or a.Item5 = '" + @iQryCond3 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond4+ "'='' or a.Item6 = '" + @iQryCond4 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond5+ "'='' or a.Item7 = '" + @iQryCond5 + "')

and " + @wSubSql

end

END

if @i1 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.MinorNm like '" + @i1 +"%' "

if @i2 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.Remark like '" + @i2 +"%' "

if @i3 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.JoinTable like '" + @i3 +"%' "

if @i4 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.JoinTable like '" + @i4 +"%' "

if @i5 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.TrunNm like '" + @i5 +"%' "

if @i6 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.Item1 like '" + @i6 +"%' "

if @i7 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.Item2 like '" + @i7 +"%' "

if @i8 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and Item3 like '" + @i8 +"%' "

if @i9 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.Item4 like '" + @i9 +"%' "

if @i10 != '' select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.Item5 like '" + @i10 +"%' "

if @iPrev = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by a.JoinTable "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " a.TrunNm " ELSE " a.MinorNm " END + " "

end

else

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by a.JoinTable desc "

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " order by " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " a.TrunNm " ELSE " a.MinorNm " END + "desc"

end

PRINT (@wSql)

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

end

/\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*/

if @iTMajorCode = 'FHF' -- 인건비예산

begin

select @wSql = "SELECT top " + convert(varchar,@iRowCount) + " BgtItemNm, '', BgtItemCd , BgtItemCd ,

BgtRankNm = (Select BgtItemNm From TGBBgtItem Where BgtItemCd = a.BgtRankCd) , BgtRankCd,

AccNm = (Select AccNm From TAAAccount Where AccCd = a.AccCd) , AccCd , BgtInItemCd , '', '', '', '','' From TGBBgtItem as a

Where InPutYn = '1' and LaborCostYn = '1' "

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1 --코드순

select @wSql = @wSql + " and BgtItemCd \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else --명칭순

select @wSql = @wSql + " and BgtItemNm \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and BgtItemCd like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and BgtItemNm like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and BgtItemCd \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and BgtItemNm \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

END

select @wSql = @wSql + " and ('" + @iQryCond2 + "'= '' or BgtObjType = '" + @iQryCond2 +"') "

select @wSql = @wSql + " Order by BgtItemCd "

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

end

b.ResultStdCd , b.ResultStdNm , a.LevNo , a.Item01 , a.Item02 , '', '', '', '','' From TGBResultStd as a

JOIN TGBResultStd as b ON a.AccYY = b.AccYY and CONVERT(CHAR(1) , CONVERT(INT , a.LevNo) + 1 ) = b.LevNo

Where a.EndType \<\> '1' "

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1 --코드순

select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.ResultStdCd \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else --명칭순

end

BEGIN

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.ResultStdCd like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.ResultStdNm like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.ResultStdCd \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.ResultStdNm \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

END

select @wSql = @wSql + " and ('" + @iQryCond1 + "'= '' or a.AccYY = '" + @iQryCond1 +"') "

select @wSql = @wSql + " Order by a.AccYY desc , a.LevNo "

exec (@wSql) set rowcount 0

return

/\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*/

if @iTMajorCode = 'FHL' -- 차수별예산

begin

select @wSql = "SELECT top " + convert(varchar,@iRowCount) + " b.BgtItemNm ,

a.AccYY , a.BgtItemCd , a.BgtItemCd , a.BgtAmdType , BgtAmdTypeNm = (Select MinorNm From TDAMinor Where MinorCd = a.BgtAmdType) ,

Convert(int,Sum(isnull(AdjTotAmt,0))),'' ,'' , '', '', '', '','' From TGBBgtM as a Left Outer Join TGBBgtItem as b ON a.AccYY = b.AccYY and a.BgtItemCd = b.BgtItemCd

Where 1 = 1 "

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1 --코드순

select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.BgtItemCd \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else --명칭순

select @wSql = @wSql + " and b.BgtItemNm \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and a.BgtItemCd like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and b.BgtItemNm like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and b.BgtItemNm \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

END

select @wSql = @wSql + " and ('" + @iQryCond1 + "'= '' or a.AccYY = '" + @iQryCond1 +"') "

select @wSql = @wSql + " and ('" + @iQryCond2 + "'= '' or a.BgtAmdType = '" + @iQryCond2 +"') "

select @wSql = @wSql + " and ('" + @iQryCond3 + "'= '' or a.RqstAccUnit = '" + @iQryCond3 +"') " --

select @wSql = @wSql + " Group by a.AccYY , a.BgtAmdType , a.BgtItemCd, b.BgtItemNm Order by a.AccYY desc , a.BgtItemCd "

exec (@wSql)

return

begin

B.SeCd, B.MokCd , '', '', '', '', '', '', '',''

From TGIIncItem B " + Char(13)

-- 부서코드가 넘어오면

--If @iQryCond4 \<\> ""

--begin

-- select @wSql = @wSql + " Inner Join TGIIncClass CM On B.AccYY = CM.AccYY And B.IncItemCd = CM.IncItemCd " + Char(13)

select @wSql = @wSql + " where 1=1 "

end

else

begin

A.MinorNm As IncItemNm

, A.Item1 As AccYY

, A.Item2 As IncItemCd

, A.Item2 As IncItemCd2

, B.IncItemNm As BgtRankNm

, A.Item4 As BgtRankCd

, ''

, A.Item6 As AccCd

, '', '', '', '', ''

from TDAMinor A

On A.Item4 = B.IncItemCd

where A.MinorCd like 'FI2%' "

end

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1 --코드순

select @wSql = @wSql + " and A.Item2 \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else --명칭순

select @wSql = @wSql + " and A.MinorNm \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and B.IncItemNm like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'" + char(13)

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

else

end

END

select @wSql = @wSql + " and ('" + @iQryCond1 + "'= '' or B.ItemType \> '" + @iQryCond1 +"') " -- 상위항목

select @wSql = @wSql + " and ('" + @iQryCond2 + "'= '' or B.InputYn = '" + @iQryCond2 +"') " -- 입력항목

If @iQryCond3 \<\> ""

begin

select @wSql = @wSql + "

and B.IncItemCd In (select ZI.IncItemCd

from TGIIncItem ZI

Inner Join TGIIncClass ZC On ZI.IncItemCd = ZC.IncItemCd

Inner Join TGIIncClassDept ZD On ZC.IncClassCd = ZD.IncClassCd

where '" + @iQryCond3 + "' = '' Or ZD.AIDeptCd = '" + @iQryCond3 + "'

group by ZI.IncItemCd)"

end

 

select @wSql = @wSql + char(13) + " Order by B.IncItemCd"

print @wSql

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

end

"

if @iTMajorCode = 'FJ1' -- 예산과목(CodeHelp)

begin

-- 조회대상 년도

declare @8J1_AccYY As varchar(4)

select @8J1_AccYY = Left(Convert(char(4), GetDate(), 112), 4)

if @iSubMajorCode = '002' -- YYYY+1

select @8J1_AccYY = Cast(Cast(@8J1_AccYY As Int) + 1 As varchar(4))

else if @iSubMajorCode = '003' -- YYYY-1

select @8J1_AccYY = Cast(Cast(@8J1_AccYY As Int) - 1 As varchar(4))

else if @iSubMajorCode = '004' -- YYYY-2

select @8J1_AccYY = Cast(Cast(@8J1_AccYY As Int) - 2 As varchar(4))

select @wSql = "

select Top " + convert(varchar,@iRowCount) + "

A.MinorNm As BgtItemNm

, A.Item1 As AccYY

, A.Item2 As BgtItemCd

, A.Item2 As BgtItemCd2

, B.BgtItemNm As BgtRankNm

, A.Item4 As BgtRankCd

, C.AccNm

, A.Item6 As AccCd

, '', '', '', '', ''

from TDAMinor A

Left Outer Join TGBBgtItem B

On A.Item1 = B.AccYY And A.Item4 = B.BgtItemCd

Left Outer Join TAAAccount C

On A.Item6 = C.AccCd

where A.MinorCd like 'FJ1%' "

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1 --코드순

select @wSql = @wSql + " and A.Item2 \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else --명칭순

select @wSql = @wSql + " and A.MinorNm \<= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

BEGIN

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and A.Item2 like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and A.MinorNm like '"+ @iStartCode +"%'"

end

else if @iOperator = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " and A.Item2 \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " and A.MinorNm \>= '"+ @iStartCode +"'"

end

END

select @wSql = @wSql + " and A.Item1 = '" + @8J1_AccYY + "' "

select @wSql = @wSql + " Order by A.Item1 desc , A.Item2 "

print @wSql

exec (@wSql)

set rowcount 0

return

end

else if @iSubMajorCode = '004' -- YYYY-2

/\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*/

else --기타

begin

-- 타 TABLE이 존제하는 거

if exists(select \* from TDAMajor with (nolock) where SourceType = 'T' and MajorCd = @iTMajorCode)

begin

select @wSql = "select MinorNm,Remark,JoinTable,JoinTable,TrunNm,

Item1,Item2,Item3,Item4,Item5,Item6,Item7,''

from TDAMinor with (nolock) "

if @iPrev = 1

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " where MinorCd like '"+ @iTMajorCode +"%' and JoinTable \<= '" + @iStartCode +"'

and ('" + @iQryCond1+ "'='' or Item3 = N'" + @iQryCond1 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond2+ "'='' or Item4 = N'" + @iQryCond2 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond3+ "'='' or Item5 = N'" + @iQryCond3 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond4+ "'='' or Item6 = N'" + @iQryCond4 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond5+ "'='' or Item7 = N'" + @iQryCond5 + "')

and " + @wSubSql

else

select @wSql = @wSql + " where MinorCd like '"+ @iTMajorCode +"%' and " + CASE @cUseTruncNmYn WHEN '1' THEN " TrunNm " ELSE " MinorNm " END + " \<= N'" + @iStartCode +"'

and ('" + @iQryCond1+ "'='' or Item3 = N'" + @iQryCond1 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond2+ "'='' or Item4 = N'" + @iQryCond2 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond3+ "'='' or Item5 = N'" + @iQryCond3 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond4+ "'='' or Item6 = N'" + @iQryCond4 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond5+ "'='' or Item7 = N'" + @iQryCond5 + "')

and " + @wSubSql

end

else

BEGIN

if @iOperator = 0

begin

if @iSortOrder = 1

select @wSql = @wSql + " where MinorCd like '"+ @iTMajorCode +"%' and JoinTable like '" + @iStartCode +"%'

and ('" + @iQryCond1+ "'='' or Item3 = N'" + @iQryCond1 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond2+ "'='' or Item4 = N'" + @iQryCond2 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond3+ "'='' or Item5 = N'" + @iQryCond3 + "')

-- 레벨코드가 다섯번째 조회조건(@iQryCond5)으로 들어옴(2000.09.19 : 홍기화)

if @iQryCond5 \> '' and (select IsLevel from TDAMajor with (nolock) where MajorCd = @iTMajorCode) \> 1

begin

if @iQryCond5 = '1' select @iQryCond5 = '', @vcQryCond5Flag = '1' -- 1레벨 조회

else if @iQryCond5 = '2' select @iQryCond5 = '', @vcQryCond5Flag = '2' -- 2레벨 전체조회

else if @iQryCond5 = '3' select @iQryCond5 = '', @vcQryCond5Flag = '3' -- 3레벨 전체조회

else

BEGIN

select @nCnt = 8

while @nCnt \> 0

BEGIN

if substring(@iQryCond5,@nCnt,1) != '0'

BEGIN

select @iQryCond5 = substring(@iQryCond5,1,@nCnt)

break

END

select @nCnt = @nCnt - 1

END

if len(@iQryCond5) \<= 2 select @vcQryCond5Flag = '2',

@iQryCond5 = substring(LTRIM(RTRIM(@iQryCond5)) + '00', 1, 2) -- 2레벨 해당건만 조회

else if len(@iQryCond5) \<= 5 select @vcQryCond5Flag = '3' --3레벨 해당건만 조회

else select @vcQryCond5Flag = '4' --전체조회

END

select @wSql = "select MinorNm,Remark,MinorCd,MinorCd,TrunNm,

Item1,Item2,Item3,Item4,Item5,Item6,Item7,LevelCd

from TDAMinor with (nolock) "

begin

select @wSql = @wSql + " where MinorCd like '"+ @iTMajorCode +"%' and MinorCd \<= '" + @iStartCode +"'

and ('" + @iQryCond1+ "'='' or Item3 = N'" + @iQryCond1 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond2+ "'='' or Item4 = N'" + @iQryCond2 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond3+ "'='' or Item5 = N'" + @iQryCond3 + "')

and ('" + @iQryCond4+ "'='' or Item6 = N'" + @iQryCond4 + "')

and ( LevelCd \> '" + @iQryCond5 + "' and LevelCd \< '" + @iQryCond5 + "zzzzzzzzz' )

and (('" + @vcQryCond5Flag + "'= '1' and substring(LevelCd,3,6) = '000000') or

('" + @vcQryCond5Flag + "'= '2' and substring(LevelCd,3,6)!= '000000' and substring(LevelCd,6,3) = '000') or

('" + @vcQryCond5Flag + "'= '3' and substring(LevelCd,6,3)!= '000') )

and " + @wSubSql

end

else

2\. 신규 코드 도움 추가 요청

     코드도움 상수 :  CSD

     코드도움명     : 거래처(삭제포함)

     거래처 상태 무시 하고 거래처 모두 나오도록 신규 코드도움 작성해 주세요.

 

CSD 부분 로직 추가

 

SELECT TOP 100 CustStatus,\* FROM TDACUSTMASTER WHERE CustStatus= 18300008

 

SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(MinorCd, ''))) FROM TDAMinor WHERE MinorCd Like '183%' And Item4 = '1'

 

SELECT \* FROM TDAMajor WHERE MajorCd = '183'

SELECT \* FROM TDAMinor WHERE MinorCd LIKE '183%'

 

 

 

select cust.CustNm,cust.Bizno_Nation,cust.CustCd,cust.CustCd,cust.TrunNm,dbo.\_FCOMDecrypt(cust.PersonId_TelexNo, 'TDACustMaster' , 'PersonId_TelexNo', 1),cust.CustomerNo,cust.Owner,'','','','',''

from TDACustMaster cust With (NoLock) left outer join TDAMinor mnr With (NoLock) on ( cust.CustStatus = mnr.MinorCd)

where ( 1=1) and cust.CustNm like N'%' and (1=1) AND cust.CustStatus =18300008 order by cust.CustNm
